NFR15 (gopter property suite asserts no injected secret, across eight shapes, survives `Redact()` over the whole `EvidencePackage` for all three role contexts). FR41 (the fifth append-only `AUDIT.redactions` subject; LimitsPolicy stream; committed JSON-Schema with closed enums). NFR18 (no secret value is ever carried on the audit event). NFR16 (append-only via LimitsPolicy). NFR35 (redact package line coverage 88.7%).
- **ADRs:** `audit.redactions.v1` registered in docs/schema-versioning.md, with the BI-2 Epic-3-vs-Epic-4 redaction reconciliation note. **eval_run_ids rationale:** a security-boundary enforcement claim proven by property tests; no Epic-5 evaluation run is required (the trust-boundary empirical work is the Epic 5 LLM evaluation, not this redaction claim).
- **Known trade-off (M1, low priority):** base64/hex identifiers (UUIDs, git SHAs) that happen to sit under `data`/`payload`/`bytes` keys are also hashed-and-dropped (the safe over-redaction direction); a later refinement may exempt canonical-id forms. Tracked, not blocking.

### `c3.6.3-llm-circuit-breaker` (Story 3.12)

- **Story:** 3.12 (LLM-tier backpressure circuit breaker; FR51 LLM half / NFR23, NFR35, NFR42). A global, per-MINUTE, BINARY-bypass breaker (`internal/decision/analyst/circuitbreaker.go`) — distinct from the per-source, per-second, sampling `internal/ratelimit.Limiter` (the sensing half), which the LLM tier does not fit. It counts LLM-eligible packages (post-FR19-gate) over a sliding 1-minute window (60 x 1s ring); when the count exceeds `rate_per_min` (default 10) it engages and the chain is BYPASSED (deterministic-only, `llm_capped_confidence` 0) until the rate stays at/below threshold for a contiguous `cooling` window (default 60s). Transition-once semantics (engage/disengage each fire the structured log + advance `EngagedTotal` once; re-engage during cooldown is a continuous engagement). Gated in `processChainPackage` AFTER `ShouldTriggerChain`, before `chain.Run` (the Story 3.11 merged FSM consumer runs this inline); a bypass records `olaitan_investigation_chain_runs_total{outcome="breaker_bypassed"}` and folds 0. `olaitan_llm_circuit_breaker_engaged_total` is bound via `NewCounterFunc` over `EngagedTotal()` (the `olaitan_sensor_circuit_breaker_engaged_total` precedent). Helm-tunable `analyst.circuit_breaker.{rate_per_min, cooling_seconds}` (AC4) bridged into the embedded config + hot-reloaded via `mgr.Subscribe` (`UpdateRatePerMin`/`UpdateCooling`/`UpdateEnabled`). A nil/disabled breaker leaves the Story 3.11 consumer byte-identical.
- **Merge SHA:** `36c7137` (PR #51, squash-merged to `epic-3-staging`).
- **Fences:** prompt ConfigMap (3.13), full audit pipeline (3.14), observability consolidation (3.15), Helm presets (3.16) untouched.

### `c3.6.3-llm-retry-fallback` (Story 3.10)

- **Story:** 3.10 (Schema validation, retry policy, and Ollama fallback; FR26/FR28, NFR35, NFR42). Each role's provider call now runs under a 3-strike retry (`internal/retry`, 1s/4s/16s base delays + jitter, `Min` 1s × `Multiplier` 4) wrapping the existing `Run`; a schema violation or transient provider error is retried, while the deterministic precondition sentinels (`ErrNoCitableEvents`/`ErrNoHypothesis`) are marked `retry.Permanent` so they fail fast and never burn a strike. On primary exhaustion a per-role Ollama fallback runner (wired in `cmd/olaitan` from `analyst.local`, shared one provider across roles; nil when the role is already Ollama or `analyst.local` is unconfigured) takes the same 3-strike retry, incrementing `olaitan_llm_fallback_total{from_provider,to_provider,role}` once at the fall-through. When primary and fallback both exhaust, the role is treated as unavailable: L1 -> skip L2 + Senior evidence-only (unchanged), L2 -> hypothesis-only (unchanged), and **Senior -> a degraded `ThreatAssessment` marked `llm_unavailable` with `llm_capped_confidence: 0`** (the Story 3.7 abort is replaced) so the FSM proceeds on the deterministic ThreatScore only (AC4). The retry + fallback nests INSIDE the Story 3.9 `l1Step`/`l2Step` (after a checkpoint miss, before the save); a checkpoint hit still skips the provider entirely (`Resumed` unchanged). With no fallback wired and a first-try success the chain is behaviourally identical to Story 3.9.
- **Merge SHA:** `11f4965` (PR #49, squash-merged to `epic-3-staging`).
- **Fences:** the score fold (Story 3.11, `score.go` LLM term stays `× 0.0`), the FSM consumption of `llm_capped_confidence`/`llm_unavailable` (3.11), the LLM-tier circuit breaker (3.12), the prompt ConfigMap (3.13), and the full audit pipeline (3.14) are untouched. The `LLMUnavailable` field is an ADDITIVE omitempty extension of `schema.ThreatAssessment` (byte-identical zero form, pinned by `TestThreatAssessmentLLMUnavailableWireForm`). **Interpretation note (DW3.10-1):** the analyst runners collapse all provider errors to `ErrProviderUnavailable` without surfacing transient-vs-permanent, so the retry treats every non-precondition provider error as retryable; a permanent error (e.g. a 401) therefore burns 3 strikes before falling through. Bounded by the provider's per-call timeout; surfacing the four-class taxonomy to skip retries on permanent-client errors is deferred.

### `c3.6.3-checkpoint-subjects-stream` + `c3.6.3-checkpoint-orchestrator` (Story 3.9)

- **Story:** 3.9 (Investigation checkpointing on NATS with restart resume; FR29, NFR35, NFR42). The chain now checkpoints L1/L2 outputs to `INVESTIGATIONS.{package_id}.{l1,l2}` (the `subjects.InvestigationL1/L2` helpers, no string literals; the package_id is `validateToken`-guarded so a hostile id cannot inject extra subject tokens) on a new `INVESTIGATIONS` JetStream stream (6h retention, architecture.md:235; Helm-tunable via `analyst.checkpoint_retention`). The orchestrator gains a `CheckpointStore` seam: `l1Step`/`l2Step` check-before-run and save-after-success per role, so a controller restart resumes from the last completed step. Resume is structural (BI-5): the consumer acks only after the chain completes, so a crash leaves the `EvidencePackage` un-acked; JetStream redelivers it and the chain re-checks the checkpoints, re-running only the un-checkpointed steps. The Senior is NOT checkpointed (a post-L2 restart re-runs only the Senior). Checkpointing is best-effort durability: a Save failure or a Load error logs and re-runs the step, never aborting. With a nil store the orchestrator is byte-identical to Story 3.8 (all 3.7/3.8 tests pass unchanged). The NATS-backed store lives in `internal/decision/analyst/checkpoint` (PublishJS with `WithMsgID` = package_id+step for idempotent re-publish; `GetLastMsgForSubject` for resume), keeping the analyst package free of the transport layer.
- **Merge SHA:** `deef5a0` (PR #48, squash-merged to `epic-3-staging`).
- **Fences:** the score fold (Story 3.11, score.go LLM term stays 0.0), retries/three-strike (3.10), and the full audit pipeline (3.14) are untouched. The `INVESTIGATIONS` stream is NOT in `auditStreamNames` (it is checkpoint/resume data, not an append-only SIEM audit subject); its retention rides a dedicated `StreamConfigsWithRetention` parameter.

### `c3.6.3-chain-trigger-ablation` + `c3.6.3-per-role-routing` + `c3.6.3-assessment-audit` (Story 3.8)

- **Story:** 3.8 (Investigation chain trigger and per-role provider routing; FR19/FR25/FR27/FR53, NFR27, NFR35, NFR42). The INTEGRATION story: it opens the `cmd/olaitan` + `internal/config` + Helm fences 3.5-3.7 kept closed and replaces the `_ = claudeProvider`/`_ = ollamaProvider` HOLD lines with a live chain. `analyst.ShouldTriggerChain` is the FR19 gate (rule severity >= 50 parsed via `strconv.Atoi`, OR any baseline sigma >= 3.0 with the score.go NaN/Inf/neg guard; a multi-signal-only package carries neither and correctly never triggers). Per-role routing (FR25): a per-role provider family {claude, openai, ollama, none, ""} + model in `internal/config`, a `cmd/olaitan` `buildRoleProvider` factory (which IMPORTS `openai_compat`, closing the previously-unreachable gap), and Helm `analyst.{l1,l2,senior}_{provider,model}` bridges. Ablation (FR53/AC4): `NewChain` accepts nil l2/senior; `l2_enabled: false` = L1-only, `senior_enabled: false` = L1+L2; the reduced assessment is built from the boundary role and capped at that role's `ScoreCap`. A new durable `olaitan-investigation-chain` consumer runs the chain ALONGSIDE the unchanged deterministic FSM consumer and publishes a minimal `audit.assessments.v1` event to the new `AUDIT.assessments` subject (AC4 `agents_available` records the ablation). The `none` mode (AC3/FR27) builds no chain and discloses rules-only at startup. The LLM score fold into the FSM ThreatScore is deliberately NOT here (Story 3.11; `score.go` LLM term stays `0.0`), so the chain runs and audits without yet moving the FSM.

Finding during implementation: Go's `encoding/json.Marshal` already HTML-escapes `<` even inside `json.RawMessage` (compact-with-escape), so the original DW3.3-1 break was not reachable on the std-marshal path; the hardening makes the guarantee locally owned and provable instead of an implicit marshaller default. The regression test was mutation-proven against a simulated non-escaping encoder (`json.Encoder` with `SetEscapeHTML(false)` plus the explicit escape removed): it failed with two closing tags and a raw `<` in the payload, then the hardening was restored and the suite went green. The `req.Schema` block is deliberately NOT escaped (repo-owned trusted content, never attacker-influenced evidence).

No `cmd/olaitan`, `internal/config` or Helm change ships in 3.5.

### `c3.6.x-prompt-configmap` (Story 3.13)

- **Story:** 3.13 (Prompt ConfigMap with hot-reload and prompt-version audit; NFR41, FR49 prompt half, NFR35). Extracts the per-role L1/L2/Senior system prompts (plus an Epic-4 DFIR placeholder) out of the Story 3.8 built-in consts into versioned files (`internal/agent/prompts/defaults/*.txt`) loaded at runtime from a Kubernetes ConfigMap mounted at `/etc/olaitan/prompts/`. A new `internal/agent/prompts.Store` mirrors the Story 1.15 rules loader (parent-dir fsnotify watch, 50 ms debounce, atomic-pointer swap, recover-on-panic subscriber fan-out) and content-hashes the canonical (newline-trimmed) prompt text; a role absent from the mount falls back to its binary-embedded default. The L1/L2/Senior runners hold the `PromptSpec` behind an `atomic.Pointer` with a `SetPrompt` swap, so a ConfigMap reload is picked up on the next call without rebuilding the chain (`Chain.SetPrompts` swaps primary + Ollama-fallback runners); the reload logs one `prompt_version_changed{role,old_hash,new_hash}` per changed role. The content hash flows to `L1Result.PromptVersion` (the field Story 3.14 publishes to `AUDIT.assessments`). A CI gate (`hack/check-prompt-changelog.sh`, wired as the `prompt-changelog` job) fails any PR that edits a prompt file without recording its new hash in `docs/prompt-changelog.md` (NFR41 prompt-drift reproducibility).
- **BI-1 invariant:** the embedded defaults reproduce the Story 3.8 `defaultL1System`/`defaultL2System`/`defaultSeniorSystem` text byte-for-byte (canonical form); only the prompt Version changes from the static `story-3.8-default.v1` string to the content hash. The prompts ConfigMap is staged into the chart by `make helm-prepare-prompts` from the same `defaults/` tree the binary embeds, so the mounted prompt and the embedded fallback are byte-identical at build time.

### `c3.9.1-eval-harness-envelope` (Story 5.1)

- **Story:** 5.1 (Evaluation harness binary and reproducibility envelope; FR53-FR54, NFR37). The FIRST story of Epic 5 (Reproducible Evaluation) and the FOUNDATION the rest of the epic fills in. It builds the FULL `cmd/olaitan-eval/` skeleton (`main.go`, `runner.go`, `manifest.go`, `README.md`, plus `main_test.go`/`manifest_test.go`) with the `Runner` orchestration loop wired in the architecture-mandated order (cluster reset -> warm -> overlay apply -> scenario -> capture -> cleanup, architecture.md:712) behind FIVE FROZEN SEAMS so Stories 5.2-5.5 fill them WITHOUT reshaping the harness. It commits the `eval/manifest.yaml` reproducibility envelope (the eight NFR37 fields), the canonical file-bytes manifest hash, the fail-closed digest-verification refusal gate, the minimal per-run `metadata.yaml`, the CLI, the `runs/<run_id>/` layout, and the N-trial loop. It changes NO `internal/` production behaviour and touches NO Claude/Anthropic/provider code.
- **Provenance (§3.9 slug-continuity):** §3.9 (evaluation methodology) is a NEW section; the matrix families top out at §3.8 (helm) and §3.7.x (forensics), so the `c3.9.1` slug is free. `report/chapter-3-methodology.md` lives in the thesis repo, not here; per the §3.6.3/§3.6.7 slug-continuity precedent, this row attaches the §3.9.1 anchor in trust until the chapter section lands. This row IS the Epic-5-opening evaluation harness + reproducibility envelope.
- **The five frozen seams (BI-1, the core deliverable):** `ClusterController` (`Reset`/`Warm`/`Cleanup`, 5.1-owned), `ConfigOverlay` (`Apply`, Story 5.3), `Scenario` (`Run`, Story 5.2), `Capturer` (`Capture`, Story 5.4), `DigestVerifier` (`Verify`, 5.1-owned, AC3). The `Runner` holds them as interface fields and drives the fixed phase order with `Cleanup` deferred (runs on any phase error, BI-2). Each seam's minimal impl (`clusterController`, `rsOverlay`, `rsScenario`, `metadataOnlyCapturer`, `imageDigestVerifier`) carries a `// Story 5.x:` TODO naming its owning story so the fill-in is mechanical and the harness shape is frozen now.
- **The manifest (BI-3, BI-4):** `eval/manifest.yaml` pins the eight NFR37 fields (image digests, Kubernetes patch version, Falco rule corpus tag, Sigma corpus git SHA, LLM model version, random seed, sysctl snapshot, cluster bootstrap script git SHA). `manifest.go` parses it into a typed `Manifest` and FAILS FAST on any missing/empty required field. The LLM model is pinned to the REAL current model `claude-opus-4-8`; the epic's `claude-opus-4-7-2026-MM-DD` placeholder never shipped and is STALE (BI-3, the ONE deviation from the verbatim AC text, surfaced in the PR body). The model string is recorded + hashed here; it is wired to the chart's `analyst.model` by Story 5.3/5.5, not here.
- **The manifest hash (BI-5):** `Manifest.SHA256()` hashes the COMMITTED file bytes (re-derivable via `sha256sum eval/manifest.yaml`), not a re-marshalled struct, so YAML key-order / comment / whitespace drift never silently changes the hash. Recorded as `manifest_sha256` in every run's `metadata.yaml`. `TestManifest_SHA256_MatchesCommittedFixture` pins `SHA256()` == `sha256sum` of the committed fixture.
- **The digest-verify gate (BI-6):** fail-CLOSED. The harness REFUSES to start (non-zero exit, a clear expected-vs-actual error) on any pinned-artefact digest mismatch OR any artefact whose actual digest cannot be resolved, UNLESS allow-listed via the documented `--allow-unverified=<name>` escape hatch (off by default, voids the reproducibility guarantee for that artefact). The AC5 minimal impl verifies the container image digest; the Falco-corpus / Sigma-SHA / bootstrap-SHA checks carry `// Story 5.x:` TODOs and are allow-listed until reachable.
- **The AC5 e2e (BI-7, BI-8, HONEST):** `tests/e2e/eval_smoke_test.go` (`//go:build e2e`) reuses the Story-1.19 `rs_smoke` kind bring-up and runs `olaitan-eval --scenario s1 --config rs --runs 1` (the RS arm = Falco-off, NO LLM, the lightest honest path), asserting the run completes, `runs/<run_id>/metadata.yaml` is present, `manifest_sha256` matches `sha256sum eval/manifest.yaml`, and the placeholder capture marker exists. `make eval-smoke` builds the binary + drives the smoke; it rides the existing CI `e2e` job alongside the RS smoke (OA5). The "all artefacts captured" clause is honestly scoped to the MINIMAL set (`metadata.yaml` + placeholder); the six-file rich artefact set is Story 5.4. The `--config rslt-full --runs 15` AC4 invocation PARSES + dispatches (covered by `TestParseFlags_Dispatch_RSLTFull`); its rich rslt-full EXECUTION awaits 5.2/5.3.
- **FRs/NFRs:** FR53-FR54 (the evaluation harness binary + the run-from-one-envelope contract); NFR37 (the eight-field reproducibility envelope + the per-run manifest hash); NFR35 (>= 80% line coverage on the hand-written `cmd/olaitan-eval/` Go). FENCED: the rich S1-S5 scenarios (5.2), the F/RSL/RSLT-full overlays (5.3), the six-file artefact capture (5.4), the full metadata schema + `analysis/analyse.py` (5.5), the FR55 adversarial experiment (5.6).
- **eval_run_ids rationale:** `n/a`. This row IS the harness; it is the PRODUCER of run ids and has none of its own to cite yet. The c3.7.5-c3.7.13 `n/a` rationale ("no Epic 5 evaluation harness yet") inverts here: not "the harness does not exist" but "this row builds it; its first cited runs land with Stories 5.2-5.6".
- **Merge SHA:** `46a4ec3` (PR [#69](https://github.com/olokotoh/olaitan/pull/69), squash-merged to `epic-5-staging` 2026-06-15; pending epic-end promotion to `main`). Two bmad-code-review rounds (Copilot not configured on this repo). A pre-review remote-CI fix landed first in `9351ad2`: the kind e2e job re-anchored a `../../`-relative manifest path against the repo root and looked two levels above it (caught only on the remote runner; the RS smoke itself passed); resolved by making the eval-smoke manifest path absolute. Round 1 (Blind Hunter SHIP-WITH-FIXES + Edge Case Hunter SHIP-WITH-FIXES + Acceptance Auditor ACCEPT; no blockers/HIGH): 4 fixes in `d008814` (actual-digest resolution so the gate emits expected-vs-actual not "unresolvable"; `--scenario`/`--config` arm validation rejecting typos; millisecond run_id + fail-closed collision guard; repeatable `--allow-unverified` + warn-on-unmatched-entry; coverage 81.5%->85.7%). Round 2 (Regression Hunter SHIP + Completeness Auditor COMPLETE-WITH-GAPS): 3 minor doc/example drifts in `5d2326e` (example run_id millis format; traceability test/file-list sync). ### `c3.9.2-attack-scenario-harnesses` (Story 5.2)

- **Story:** 5.2 (Five attack scenario harnesses, S1-S5; FR53). The SECOND story of Epic 5 (Reproducible Evaluation); it fills the `Scenario` seam Story 5.1 froze. It delivers five `deploy/demo/scenarios/sN-<slug>/` harness directories (each with `manifests/` (the tenant-namespace target workload), `attack/inject.md` (the real-attacker description + the deterministic synthetic-event field shapes), `README.md` (the MITRE technique + success criterion + triggering rule(s)), and `target.yaml` (the target FSM state + time-to-detect)), the `Scenario`-seam fill-in in `cmd/olaitan-eval/` (a scenario factory dispatching `--scenario sN` to the matching harness, threaded into `newRunner`, replacing the 5.1 `rsScenario` no-op), and the `tests/e2e/scenarios_smoke_test.go` kind test. It changes NO `internal/` production behaviour (the rules + baseline + correlator + FSM are already shipped) and touches NO Claude/Anthropic/provider code.
- **Provenance (§3.9 slug-continuity):** §3.9.2 is the next consecutive evaluation-methodology slug after §3.9.1; the matrix families top out at §3.9.1, so the `c3.9.2` slug is free. `report/chapter-3-methodology.md` lives in the thesis repo, not here; per the §3.6.3/§3.6.7 + §3.9.1 slug-continuity precedent, this row attaches the §3.9.2 anchor in trust until the chapter section lands.
- **BI-1 directory-naming reconciliation (surfaced):** the epic AC text says `deploy/demo/scenarios/{s1..s5}/` (bare short ids); the architecture names the SAME directories with descriptive slugs `s1-container-escape/`, `s2-credential-exfil/`, `s3-lateral-movement/`, `s4-c2-beaconing/`, `s5-cryptomining/`. 5.2 takes the architecture's descriptive slugs (self-documenting, the canonical project-structure pin) and resolves each from the short `--scenario sN` id via the deterministic `scenarioSlugs` map in `cmd/olaitan-eval/scenario.go`. This is the ONE deviation from the verbatim epic text, surfaced in the PR body.
- **The scenario -> rule mapping (firm by corpus read):** S1 T1611 -> OLT-PRIV-001; S2 T1552 -> OLT-CRED-001/OLT-CRED-002; S3 T1613 -> OLT-LATERAL-001; S4 T1071 -> OLT-NET-001/OLT-NET-002 (and/or the `outbound_unique_dst_ips` baseline deviation, BI-4); S5 T1496 -> OLT-IMPACT-005/OLT-IMPACT-006. Each scenario's deterministic stimulus emits the EXACT field shape the rule's `detection:` block matches.
- **The determinism mechanism (BI-3):** the rs_smoke precedent. Falco's eBPF probe cannot load inside a kind node, so each scenario's kind stimulus is a DETERMINISTIC synthetic-event injection to `olaitan.events.raw.{falco,network}` (the subjects the production Falco / CNI exporters publish to), with a priming event of a DIFFERENT source than the match event so the correlator's two-distinct-source rising edge assembles the EvidencePackage the rules/baseline engines evaluate. The field shapes are the single source of truth in `cmd/olaitan-eval/scenario.go` (`scenarioEvents`), unit-tested for byte-stability across two invocations modulo the injection timestamp (AC7); the e2e injector mirrors them. No rand, no `time.Now()`-keyed branching, no real exploit, no external network.
- **AC8 honest scope (BI-8):** `tests/e2e/scenarios_smoke_test.go` (`//go:build e2e`) reuses the Story-1.19 rs_smoke RS bring-up and, per scenario, asserts a rule match OR baseline deviation reaches `EVIDENCE.packages` (`olaitan_decision_rules_matches_by_attribute_total{rule_id=...}` >= 1 OR `olaitan_decision_baseline_deviations_total` >= 1, AND `olaitan_correlator_evidence_packages_total` >= 1) within each scenario's `target.yaml` time-to-detect window, plus an idempotency re-run (AC7). AC8 asserts the EVIDENCE-package SIGNAL, NOT the full FSM-state attainment of AC2-AC6 (the QUARANTINED / RESTRICTED / SUSPICIOUS targets + the `measured_time_to_detect` are Story 5.4 + the carry-forward Epic-3-retro A1 RSLT-full-kind gate). `make scenarios-smoke` mirrors `make eval-smoke` for local runs.
- **Review Round 2 (CI-caught, second pass):** the live kind smoke failed flakily two ways across reruns. (1) SHARED-WORKLOAD RISING-EDGE FRAGILITY: the correlator publishes an EvidencePackage only on the per-workload rising edge of multi-signal convergence (`internal/correlator/window/window.go:171-185`, `fired` map keyed on `workloadID`; `internal/correlator/correlator.go:255-257` early-returns when `transitioned=false`), and `workloadID = tenant-acme/Deployment/<owner-name>` (`internal/keys/workload.go:36` + `internal/correlator/correlator.go:395`) is derived from the OWNING Deployment, not the pod. All scenarios + rs_smoke + the S4 pre-seed originally shared `tenant-acme/Deployment/web`, so once the FIRST converger (rs_smoke, or an earlier subtest) set `fired` within the still-open 60s window, later scenarios got `transitioned=false` and a fresh-package DELTA=0. FIX: each scenario now uses its OWN tenant-acme Deployment (`scenario-<id>`, idempotency test `scenario-idem-s1`) via a new `applyScenarioWorkload(t, deployName)` and a `deployName`-parameterised `publishSyntheticEvidencePackagesFor` (so S4's baseline pre-seed lands on the scenario's own workload key); all OLT rules key on namespace + owner_kind, NOT the Deployment name (verified across `rules/{priv,cred,lateral,net,impact}/*.yaml`), so every rule still matches. The shared recipe `internal/eval/scenario/recipes.go` is unchanged (it already parameterises on `podName`; the in-process binary keeps its default). (2) AGGREGATOR EVENT-LOSS FLAKINESS: on the constrained single-node kind cluster the heavy aggregator restarts ~2x during startup on a NATS-not-ready race, dropping published events (S2's observed `rule_matches=0` is consistent). FIX: the 5-scenario + idempotency smoke moves OUT of the always-on CI `e2e` job into a new OPT-IN `e2e-scenarios` job gated by the `e2e-scenarios` PR label (mirroring `e2e-forensics`); Story 5.1's stable gate (the RS smoke + `TestEvalSmoke_S1_RS_OneTrial`) STAYS always-on. The deterministic full run folds into the carry-forward A1 3-node cluster gate. This is honest scoping consistent with BI-8 and the A1 carry-forward, NOT a silent lowering of the bar.
- **FRs/NFRs:** FR53 (the five MITRE-annotated attack scenarios as comparable stimuli). NFR35 (>= 80% line coverage on the hand-written `cmd/olaitan-eval/` Go; the scenario factory + determinism tests keep the package at 87.5%). FENCED: the six config-matrix overlays (Story 5.3 - 5.2 runs the existing RS arm), the six-file artefact capture (Story 5.4), the `success_criterion_met`/`measured_time_to_detect` metadata (Story 5.4/5.5), the statistical pipeline (5.5), the FR55 adversarial experiment (5.6).
- **eval_run_ids rationale:** `n/a`. The scenarios PRODUCE stimuli for runs but have no cited run ids of their own yet; the §3.9.1 `n/a` rationale continues (the first cited run ids land with Story 5.4/5.5/5.7).
- **Merge SHA:** `1e8ccc5` (PR [#70](https://github.com/olokotoh/olaitan/pull/70), squash-merged to `epic-5-staging` 2026-06-15; pending epic-end promotion to `main`). Two bmad-code-review rounds (Copilot not configured on this repo). Round 1 (Acceptance Auditor CLEAN + Edge Case Hunter NON-BLOCKING + Blind Hunter NON-BLOCKING): fixes in `a3d9f3a` (per-scenario counter DELTA in the e2e assertion so each scenario proves its OWN rule match/baseline deviation rather than passing on an earlier scenario's residue; poll-budget cap; nil-logger guard; recipes extracted to the shared non-main package `internal/eval/scenario`). Round 2 (Regression Hunter NON-BLOCKING + Completeness Verifier one traceability BLOCKER): `994b480` added `recipes.go` to this row, snapshotted the S4 delta before the pre-seed, and matched the rs_smoke 90s poll budget. Live kind CI then caught two real fragilities the host could not: (1) the idempotency re-run required a fresh correlator package the rising-edge gate does not emit for a same-workload repeat (`9ca0b35` scopes run 2 to re-detection); (2) all five scenarios + rs_smoke shared the `tenant-acme/Deployment/web` workload key so only the first convergence emitted a package, and the constrained single-node kind aggregator drops events on its startup restart race (`111f356` gives each scenario its own Deployment for an independent rising-edge key, and moves the heavy 5-scenario smoke into an opt-in `e2e-scenarios` label-gated job mirroring `e2e-forensics`; the deterministic full run folds into the A1 cluster gate). Story 5.1's RS smoke + eval-smoke stay always-on. CI green (go, helm, docker, e2e incl. the kind RS+eval smoke, the four integration jobs, traceability, prompt-changelog) on the merged SHA.

### `c3.9.4-per-run-artefact-capture` (Story 5.4)

- **Story:** 5.4 (Per-run artefact capture; FR54). The FOURTH story of Epic 5 (Reproducible Evaluation); it fills the `Capturer` seam Story 5.1 froze (`cmd/olaitan-eval/runner.go`, replacing the `metadataOnlyCapturer` placeholder). It delivers a rich Capturer (in the importable `internal/eval/capture` package + a thin `cmd/olaitan-eval` adapter behind the frozen interface) that SUBSCRIBES to the run's NATS subjects and SERIALISES what it observes into the UNIFORM six-artefact set under `runs/<run_id>/`: `events.jsonl`, `evidence.jsonl`, `assessments.jsonl`, `fsm.jsonl`, `report.md`, `metadata.yaml`. It changes NO `internal/` production behaviour (it only drains subjects that already exist + carry payloads) and touches NO Claude/Anthropic/provider code.
- **Provenance (§3.9 slug-continuity):** §3.9.4 is the next consecutive evaluation-methodology slug after §3.9.3; the matrix families top out at §3.9.3, so the `c3.9.4` slug is free (VERIFIED FREE: traceability had c3.9.1/c3.9.2/c3.9.3 only, no c3.9.4). `report/chapter-3-methodology.md` lives in the thesis repo, not here; per the §3.6.3/§3.6.7 + §3.9.1/§3.9.2/§3.9.3 slug-continuity precedent, this row attaches the §3.9.4 anchor in trust until the chapter section lands.
- **BI-1 SUBJECT-NAME reconciliation (the LOAD-BEARING correction, surfaced):** the epic AC1 text names `EVENTS.raw` / `EVIDENCE.packages` / `ASSESSMENTS.completed`; the REAL `internal/subjects/` constants are used instead (NEVER hard-coded subject strings): `events.jsonl` <- `subjects.RawPrefix + ">"` (`olaitan.events.raw.>`, the `EVENTS_RAW` stream), `evidence.jsonl` <- `subjects.EvidencePackages` (`olaitan.evidence.packages`, `EVIDENCE`), `assessments.jsonl` <- `subjects.AuditAssessments` (`AUDIT.assessments`, `AUDIT_ASSESSMENTS`), `fsm.jsonl` <- `subjects.AuditTransitions` (`AUDIT.transitions`, `AUDIT_TRANSITIONS`), `report.md` <- `subjects.ReportsGenerated` (`REPORTS.generated`, `REPORTS`) + the S3 fetch. The artefact FILE names stay exactly as FR54 names them; only the SOURCE-subject names are reconciled. This is the ONE deviation from the verbatim epic text.
- **BI-2 single-source assessments (no `ASSESSMENTS.completed` exists):** a repo-wide search finds NO `ASSESSMENTS.completed` subject (no const, no publisher, no stream); the LLM-verdict record is published ONLY to `AUDIT.assessments` (the Story-3.8 synchronous publisher). So the epic's "`ASSESSMENTS.completed` AND `AUDIT.assessments`" collapses to the single real subject and dedup is moot. `assessments.jsonl` sources `subjects.AuditAssessments` alone (OQ1: confirm single-source vs a future second subject for a later story).
- **BI-4 the self-describing JSONL envelope:** each `.jsonl` line is `{schema_version, published_at, subject, payload}` (envelope contract `olaitan.eval.capture.envelope.v1`, recorded in `docs/schema-versioning.md`). `schema_version` identifies the SOURCE contract (`event.v1` / `evidence_package.v1` / `audit.assessments.v1` / `audit.transitions.v1`); `published_at` is the JetStream message publish/observation time; `payload` is the VERBATIM decoded source body, preserved so Story 5.5's pandas pipeline reads ONE shape across all 400 runs. 5.4 wraps the existing wire payloads; it does NOT re-shape or re-validate them (the payload's own `schema_version` is preserved inside).
- **BI-5 the honest `report.md` (no S3):** `REPORTS.generated` carries the report SHA256 + content-addressed URL, NOT the body (the durable S3-compatible write is Story 4.6). `report.md` is assembled from the announcement header; when no S3 is configured (the in-process AC5 test) or no report was generated (the RS arm), it carries the available metadata or an explicit "no report generated / no S3" note. The file ALWAYS exists (AC5) and NEVER pretends a body was fetched (OQ3: confirmed this satisfies "all six artefacts exist").
- **BI-7 metadata keep-and-add (the 5.5 contract):** `metadata.yaml` KEEPS the Story-5.1 yaml keys (`run_id`, `manifest_sha256`, `scenario`, `config`, `runs`, `started_at`, `finished_at`, `olaitan_eval_version` -- 5.5 builds on them) and ADDS the AC3 fields `success_criterion_met`, `measured_time_to_detect`, `measured_final_fsm_state`, `fsm_state_source`, `resource_usage`, `size_bytes`, `size_cap_exceeded`. The epic AC3 names (`manifest_hash`, `config_name`, `scenario_id`, `runs_in_batch`, `start_at`/`end_at`) are PROSE LABELS for the kept 5.1 keys, NOT second fields (renaming would break the 5.1 schema 5.5 consumes; OQ4 confirmed keep-and-add).
- **BI-8 honesty fences (the Story-5.2 BI-8 carry-forward):** (1) RESOURCE USAGE = the harness process's own snapshot (`runtime.MemStats` alloc/total-alloc/sys/GC + goroutines) ALWAYS; cluster snapshots are captured WHEN AVAILABLE and recorded with `cluster_metrics_available: false` + nil cluster fields otherwise, NEVER a fabricated number. (2) FSM STATE: `measured_final_fsm_state` is the highest observed `AUDIT.transitions` after-state when a transition was captured (`fsm_state_source: observed_transitions`); when none was captured but an EvidencePackage signal was observed it is the DETECTION-SIGNAL state (`fsm_state_source: detection_signal_only`); the full FSM-state attainment proof is deferred to the carry-forward A1 RSLT-full-kind gate (OQ5 confirmed).
- **BI-9 MEASURE against Story 5.2's `target.yaml`:** `success_criterion_met` / `measured_time_to_detect` / `measured_final_fsm_state` are computed by comparing the captured signal against the run's scenario `target.yaml` (the `scenarioTarget` the harness already resolves, threaded into the metadata computation via `captureTarget`). `success_criterion_met = (floor ? observed >= target : observed == target) AND ttd <= target_ttd AND detected`; the FSM ordering for the floor comparison mirrors `internal/schema/state.go` (SUSPICIOUS < RESTRICTED < QUARANTINED < PRESERVED_KILLED), pinned by `TestCaptureFSMOrder_MatchesSchema`. 5.2 DECLARES; 5.4 MEASURES. A failed/undetected run records `success_criterion_met: false` + the never-detected sentinel `-1` (a detection miss is a DATA POINT, finalised on success OR failure, BI-6).
- **BI-10 the size bound (fail-LOUD):** at finalise the run dir is summed; `size_bytes` is recorded. When over `--max-run-size-bytes` (default 500 MiB) a structured `slog.Error` alert is emitted AND `size_cap_exceeded: true` is recorded so the alert is durable. The cap is fail-OPEN on the artefacts (nothing is deleted or truncated) and fail-LOUD on the signal. A typical run is well under 50 MB.
- **BI-11 the always-on embedded-NATS in-process AC5 test (in-process vs kind decision):** AC5 is proven by `internal/eval/capture/capture_integration_test.go` (`//go:build integration`), an EMBEDDED-NATS in-process test that starts an in-process JetStream server with the run's streams (MemoryStorage, tiny MaxBytes; the `settling_integration_test.go` idiom), wires the rich Capturer, publishes a deterministic S5+RS stimulus to the REAL subjects, runs the capture, and asserts all six artefacts exist, every `.jsonl` line `json.Unmarshal`s into the envelope, `metadata.yaml`'s `manifest_sha256` == `sha256sum eval/manifest.yaml`, and the size-cap alert trips on a synthetic over-cap run (the "not silent" proof). It runs in the ALWAYS-ON `integration-inproc` CI job (`./internal/eval/capture/...` appended) WITHOUT a kind cluster, so AC5 is proven on every PR. Any kind exercise of the full capture against a real RS deployment is opt-in / label-gated (the `e2e-scenarios` precedent) or folded into the carry-forward A1 RSLT-full-kind gate, disclosed honestly; it is NOT required for AC5. The default `make eval-smoke` runs the harness WITHOUT `--nats-url`, so the six artefacts exist (empty `.jsonl` + honest no-report `report.md`) honestly.
- **FRs/NFRs:** FR54 (the uniform six-artefact set the analysis pipeline consumes without per-run special-cases). NFR37 (the captured `metadata.yaml` carries the manifest pin). NFR35 (>= 80% line coverage on the hand-written Go; the capture package + the cmd adapter tests). FENCED: the statistical analysis pipeline `analysis/analyse.py` (Story 5.5 -- 5.4 PRODUCES the artefacts 5.5 CONSUMES + extends the metadata schema), the FR55 adversarial experiment (5.6), the full FSM-state attainment proof (the A1 RSLT-full-kind gate). NO `internal/` production behaviour changed; NO new third-party module.

- **Provenance (§3.9 slug-continuity):** §3.9.3 is the next consecutive evaluation-methodology slug after §3.9.2; the matrix families top out at §3.9.2, so the `c3.9.3` slug is free (VERIFIED: only §3.9.1 and §3.9.2 exist). `report/chapter-3-methodology.md` lives in the thesis repo, not here; per the §3.6.3/§3.6.7 + §3.9.1/§3.9.2 slug-continuity precedent, this row attaches the §3.9.3 anchor in trust until the chapter section lands.

Each overlay sets ONLY `evaluation.config: <ARM>` (plus the LLM-arm model pin); it does NOT restate the per-arm knobs because under a named arm the chart CLOBBERS the operator's individual knobs anyway, so restating them is dead weight that risks drift. No new chart value knob is added; 5.3 SELECTS the existing matrix via overlay files. Verified arm->knob mapping (direct read of `values.yaml` / `EVAL.md` / `_helpers.tpl`): F = rules/baselines off, provider none, non-Falco sources auto-off via `isFArm`; RS = rules/baselines on, provider none; RSL = api, l2 off, senior on; RSLT-full = api, l2 on, senior on; RSLT-L1-only = api, l2 off, senior on; RSLT-L1+L2 = api, l2 on, senior off.
- **BI-1 filename reconciliation (surfaced):** the epic AC text says `values-eval-{config}.yaml` for the six arms. The first four map cleanly; the `RSLT-L1+L2` arm's `+` is legal but awkward in a filename, so the file is `values-eval-rslt-l1-l2.yaml` (the `+` normalised to `-` in the FILENAME ONLY). The IN-FILE `evaluation.config` stays the canonical `RSLT-L1+L2` verbatim so the chart validator + effective-value helpers fire unchanged. The harness `--config rslt-l1-l2` -> `values-eval-rslt-l1-l2.yaml` -> `evaluation.config: RSLT-L1+L2` is the single deterministic translation. This is the ONE deviation from a literal substitution, surfaced in the PR body.

The F/RS overlays carry no model pin (their provider is none). The `analyst.api.endpoint` / `analyst.api.apiKeySecret` stay at chart defaults as documented install-time pointers.
- **BI-4 validateConfig tightened:** Story 5.1's `validateConfig` accepted any open `rslt-` prefix as a forward-compat placeholder. 5.3 owns the ablation arms now, so the validator accepts EXACTLY the six arms with a committed overlay (`f`, `rs`, `rsl`, `rslt`/`rslt-full`, `rslt-l1-only`, `rslt-l1-l2`, case-insensitively); `rslt-bogus` / `rslt-l1` now fail fast at flag-parse. `overlayFileFor` ALSO fails closed for an unmapped arm and `helmOverlay.Apply` fails closed on a missing overlay file on disk (defence in depth).

CHART-WIRING RECONCILIATION (verified, refines the BI-5 literal string): the aggregator deployment is `<fullname>-aggregator` where `olaitan.fullname` dedups the chart name, so the canonical `--release olaitan` resolves to `deploy/olaitan-aggregator` (NOT `deploy/olaitan-olaitan-aggregator`); `aggregatorDeployName` mirrors the chart's fullname helper so the Ready gate names the deployment the chart ACTUALLY renders. The shell-out is injectable (`overlayRunFunc`) so the unit tests assert the exact argv without a cluster.

VERIFIED FINDING: RSL and RSLT-L1-only render BYTE-IDENTICAL (the chart does not echo the arm label into the rendered config; both have api / l2 off / senior on); their goldens are committed separately for matrix clarity and `TestGoldenFile_Eval_RSL_RSLTL1Only_Identical` pins the byte-identity (the arm distinction lives in the harness `--config` name + the run id, not the rendered config).

### `c3.9.8-preregistration` (Story 5.8)

- **Story:** 5.8 (Pre-registered analysis plan; the analytic-flexibility defence for the academic contribution). A DOCUMENTATION / PROCESS story: it commits the canonical, frozen-before-runs analysis plan so the four-way comparison, the RSLT ablation, the FR55 trust-bound test, and the DFIR rubric study are confirmatory rather than exploratory. It writes NO Go production code, calls NO provider, defines NO schema, and changes NO `internal/`/Helm behaviour. It seeds the NEW `analysis/` directory (BI-2; Story 5.5 later adds `analyse.py` + `output/` to the same directory) with `preregistration.md` (the eight mandated sections) + `README.md` (the canonical-plan declaration), plus the lightest honest structural gate and this traceability row.
- **PULLED FORWARD (epics.md:2282):** although numbered 5.8, this story MUST be completed BEFORE Stories 5.6 (FR55) and 5.7 (DFIR rubric) main runs begin, and conceptually before 5.5's analysis is interpreted (pre-registration after the fact is not pre-registration). The autonomous Epic-5 order is 5.1 -> 5.2 -> 5.3 -> 5.4 -> 5.8 -> 5.5 -> 5.6 -> 5.7; the numerical 5.8 label is preserved for thematic grouping with the analysis pipeline.
- **The eight mandated sections (BI-3):** (1) metadata (`soft_freeze_date: 2026-08-15`, the pinned model `claude-opus-4-8` per eval/manifest.yaml:43, the manifest-hash discipline, the canonical-plan declaration); (2) RQ1-RQ5 (BI-4, reconstructed from prd.md:102,317-324 since the PRD labels only RQ5 inline); (3) per-RQ H0/H1 with the PRD effect-size thresholds (RQ1 >= 15 pp DR gap + ATT&CK kappa >= 0.75, RQ2 within-window >= 80% per cell, RQ3 < 2 escalations/hour, RQ4 ablation L2 >= 10% / Senior >= 5% + FR55 0/100 bound max ThreatScore <= 10.5 Claude / <= 7.5 Ollama, RQ5 >= 3 of 5 rubric dims p<0.05 + ICC >= 0.70); (4) primary metrics (Detection Rate, MTTD, FPR, ATT&CK Cohen's kappa, the five DFIR Likert dims); (5) the per-test alpha + Bonferroni STRUCTURE table (McNemar+Bonferroni per-scenario four-way family SEPARATE from the ablation family; Kruskal-Wallis omnibus + Dunn's-with-Holm for MTTD; Wilcoxon signed-rank per rubric dimension; ICC(2,k); Cohen's kappa; a one-sided Poisson rate test for the RQ3 FPR equivalence / non-inferiority check against the 2-per-hour tolerance bound (family of the 2 LLM-bearing configs RSL/RSLT-full, 0.05/2 = 0.025); alpha 0.05 field-standard, two-sided for the directional superiority tests and one-sided for the FPR equivalence test); (6) sample sizes (N=15 main per cell + N=10 ablation per cell = 400 total; FR55 100 trials per LLM config recorded SEPARATELY, not folded into N=15, BI-8; RQ5 N=10 incidents x N>=3 raters); (7) the fixed-N no-optional-stopping rule; (8) the confirmatory test registry (stable `test_id`s, the machine-readable list Story 5.5 consumes) + the AC3 exploratory:true contract (written here, ENFORCED in Story 5.5's analyse.py) + the AC4 traceability contract.
- **DERIVED not invented (BI-1):** every hypothesis, threshold, alpha, sample size, and test traces to its PRD/epics source cited inline (prd.md:104-126,149,317-324,110,544; epics.md:2200,2206-2208,2232,2238,2258,2264). Two documented reconciliations: the MTTD post-hoc is pre-registered as Dunn's-with-Holm (the more specific prd.md:122 over epics.md:2200's bare "Dunn's", BI-7); the rubric dimensions are the five canonical PRD dims Clarity / Completeness / ATT&CK coverage / Kill-chain reconstruction accuracy / Actionability (prd.md:112,324) over Story 5.7's four-item prose (BI-7). The alpha = 0.05 is recorded as a field-standard pre-specified choice (two-sided for the directional superiority tests, one-sided for the RQ3 FPR equivalence test), not a PRD-stated value (the PRD names the tests + corrections but not a literal numeric alpha, OQ1).
- **The structural gate (BI-9, OQ6 recommended):** a `make prereg-check` target driving `hack/check-preregistration.sh`, a grep-based assertion (mirroring `hack/check-prompt-changelog.sh`) that `analysis/preregistration.md` carries the eight mandated section headings plus the confirmatory test registry marker; it fails non-zero if any is missing. Wired as a fast step into the EXISTING always-on `go` CI job (NO new job, NO cluster, NO markdown parser). The gate proves STRUCTURE, NOT statistical correctness (a human supervisor reviews the numbers); verified to PASS on the committed document and to FAIL when a mandated heading is removed (the negative proof).
- **The exploratory:true seam (BI-11):** this row commits the CONTRACT and the enumerable registry; Story 5.5's `analysis/analyse.py` OWNS the enforcement (it reads the registry and stamps any test/metric whose `test_id` is not in it as `exploratory: true` in a separate output section). The test choices here (McNemar+Bonferroni / Kruskal-Wallis+Dunn's-with-Holm / Wilcoxon / ICC(2,k)) align exactly with Story 5.5's pipeline (epics.md:2200) so the plan and the code cannot drift.
- **Provenance (§3.9.8 story-number-aligned, BI-14):** the claim is `c3.9.8-preregistration` at §3.9.8 (VERIFIED FREE: the matrix carries c3.9.1/c3.9.2/c3.9.3/c3.9.4 only, no §3.9.8). §3.9.5, §3.9.6, §3.9.7 are RESERVED-not-yet-claimed for Stories 5.5/5.6/5.7 (not yet built in this pulled-forward order). §3.9.8 is chosen to keep the §3.9.x = Story-5.x mapping (5.1->§3.9.1, 5.2->§3.9.2, 5.3->§3.9.3, 5.4->§3.9.4, 5.8->§3.9.8) rather than the next-consecutive-integer rule the earlier rows used (which only worked because they were built in order; 5.8 is pulled forward, so the story-number mapping is the non-conflicting choice, OQ8).

The forward contract (AC4): every later Chapter 3/4 claim depending on a confirmatory test cites this prereg's `test_id` row (`analysis/preregistration.md#<lowercased test_id>`) alongside the actual eval run ids; those per-claim citation rows land with Stories 5.5/5.6/5.7/5.9 as the numbers are produced. ### `c3.9.7-rubric-study` (Story 5.7)

- **Story:** 5.7 (DFIR rubric study harness, RQ5). Ships the OFFLINE rubric harness: a deterministic synthetic-S1-incident generator, the two report-variant generators (variant (a) the Story-4.4 Olaitan DFIR LLM report reused READ-ONLY, variant (b) a NEW no-LLM templated baseline over the same evidence), a seeded blind-randomised pairing, the `rubric.score.v1` scores emitter (one record per `(incident, variant, rater, dimension)` Likert score under the reserved `runs/rubric/` sub-tree + a sibling `metadata.yaml`), a static-file rater workflow (the blinded reports + per-rater scoring sheets), a thin `cmd/olaitan-rubric` CLI, the committed rubric DEFINITION/criteria/IRB-template doc, and the Story-5.5 Python consumer wiring (`analyse.build_rubric_rows` now READS the scores via the new `io.load_rubric_scores` reader). The two variants render the SAME factual sections over the SAME incident evidence; the (a)-vs-(b) contrast is the analyst narrative (variant (a) carries the LLM narrative, variant (b) a fixed templated stub).
- **THE CODE-ONLY-DEFER FENCE (BI-2, the user's load-bearing scope decision):** 5.7's done-ness is the HARNESS + the two variant generators + the blind-pairing + the scores-artefact format + the static-file workflow + the committed rubric DEFINITION + the OFFLINE end-to-end proof against a SINGLE SYNTHETIC S1 incident (both variants generated byte-deterministically, a parseable artefact with clearly-SYNTHETIC placeholder rater scores, `build_rubric_rows` computes the five Wilcoxon tests + ICC(2,k) WITHOUT crashing, degrading honestly at tiny n). The REAL rater study (N>=3 human raters x N=10 cluster-captured incidents per scenario) needs real raters + cluster-captured incidents and is a DEFERRED carry-forward, NOT part of this story's done-ness (mirrors Story 5.6 BI-2 / Story 5.5 BI-7). The harness DEGRADES HONESTLY: every offline record carries `synthetic: true` / `rater: synthetic-*` under a `rubric-offline-` run dir, NEVER a thesis-final RQ5 number; `build_rubric_rows` keeps its honest `n=0` skip until scores exist. The offline data-path result (the proof that ships now): the five Wilcoxon rows compute with n=1 paired observation per dimension (a single-incident sample, p=1.0 from scipy, a degenerate-but-real result, NOT a fabricated significant finding) and the ICC row computes with n=20 ratings across 2 synthetic raters x 10 targets (ICC point estimate 0.6); the data PATH generate -> blind-pair -> collect -> parse -> Wilcoxon+ICC is validated end-to-end.
- **OQ resolutions (the orchestrator's decisions, implemented):** OQ1 = 5.7 DID extend `build_rubric_rows` in THIS PR to READ the scores via the new `load_rubric_scores` reader and FEED the existing `tests.wilcoxon_signed_rank` + `tests.icc_2k` helpers (the paired LLM-vs-templated vectors per dimension, paired on incident_id with the rater dimension averaged; the long-form ICC ratings frame keyed on `incident_id|variant|dimension` x rater), proven end-to-end on the committed offline synthetic fixture; the byte-deterministic golden was regenerated (the six rubric rows now populate from the offline scores instead of skipping at n=0; a six-row diff in `summary.csv` + `summary.md`, total_runs unchanged at 124 because the rubric sub-tree is not a top-level run dir). OQ2 = a NEW `internal/eval/rubric/` package + a thin `cmd/olaitan-rubric/` CLI (the cluster-driven `cmd/olaitan-eval` Runner loop is NOT inherited; the frozen `dfir.ForensicReport.Render` + `schema.EvidencePackage` + `settling.IncidentFinalised` seams are reused). OQ3 = a STATIC-FILE rater workflow (blinded reports + per-rater scoring sheets), NO live web UI. OQ4 = the reserved `runs/rubric/<run-dir>/` sub-tree with a `scores.jsonl` (`rubric.score.v1` per-(incident,variant,rater,dimension) records) + a sibling `metadata.yaml` (`scenario`, `manifest_sha256`, `n_incidents`, `n_raters`, `synthetic`); `load_run_set` skips it (no top-level metadata.yaml) so the rubric scenario does not orphan in the 4x5 grid. OQ5 = the blind-pairing RNG is seeded from the incident_id (FNV-1a, a local `math/rand` source, NOT global state or `time.Now()`), so the blinded slot order + the offline golden are byte-reproducible.
- **VARIANT (a) READ-ONLY REUSE (BI-1, no additive export needed):** variant (a) is rendered by the FROZEN `dfir.ForensicReport.Render(evt)` (`internal/report/dfir/report.go`); `ForensicReport` and `Render` are ALREADY exported with public fields, so NO additive export to `internal/report/dfir` was required. The harness constructs a `ForensicReport` with a fixed SYNTHETIC narrative (a Go literal in `internal/eval/rubric/variants.go`, NEVER under `internal/agent/prompts/defaults/`) and calls `Render` with NO live LLM call (like Story 5.6's fake provider). Story-4.4 DFIR behaviour is UNCHANGED.
- **THE PROMPT-ISOLATION (BI-3, changelog gate stays green):** 5.7 makes NO LLM call offline and touches NO `internal/agent/prompts/defaults/*.txt` (incl. `dfir.txt`), so the NFR41 `hack/check-prompt-changelog.sh` gate (which watches ONLY that dir) stays green. The synthetic narrative + the templated stub are harness-only Go literals in the rubric package, unreachable from any production deploy path.
- **THE 5-OVER-4 DIMENSION RECONCILIATION (BI-5):** the confirmatory family is the FIVE canonical PRD dimensions (Clarity, Completeness, ATT&CK coverage, Kill-chain reconstruction accuracy, Actionability), keyed EXACTLY as the Story-5.5 registry (`clarity`/`completeness`/`attack-coverage`/`killchain`/`actionability`, ids `RQ5-RUBRIC-WILCOXON-{...}` + `RQ5-ICC`), per the pre-registration's reconciliation of the PRD's 5 over the epic's 4-item prose. The family is family=5 (Bonferroni 0.05/5 = 0.01). No 6th "overall usefulness" dimension is added to the confirmatory family (it would break the correction); the rubric DEFINITION doc records it as EXPLORATORY-only. The reader REJECTS any unknown dimension key (a drift guard so the Go emitter and the Python reader cannot diverge).
- **THE FED-NOT-FORKED DISCIPLINE (BI-4):** 5.7 does NOT modify the Story-5.5 `tests.wilcoxon_signed_rank` / `tests.icc_2k` helpers (they already degrade honestly at tiny/zero n); it FEEDS them the pivoted offline-synthetic data. It does NOT build a parallel statistics pipeline.
- **DEFERRED CARRY-FORWARD (BI-2):** the real rater study (N>=3 human raters x N=10 cluster-captured incidents per scenario) needs real raters + cluster-captured incidents. It is tracked here as a carry-forward (mirroring the Story-5.6 deferred-100-trial-run + the A1 RSLT-full-kind-e2e gate convention): the same `runs/rubric/<run-dir>/` artefact shape, `build_rubric_rows` reads it unchanged, and the offline synthetic set is replaced by the real anonymous rater scores. This is NOT part of 5.7's done-ness.

### `c3.9.6-fr55-bound` (Story 5.6)

- **Story:** 5.6 (Adversarial-LLM bound test, FR55). Ships the OFFLINE Trust-Bounded LLM Integration bound-test harness: a benign-input generator, a harness-only adversarial L1/L2/Senior prompt set, an in-process driver over the REAL Story-3.7 `analyst.Chain` (L1 -> L2 -> Senior) + the Story-2.1/3.11 `score.Calculator`, a deterministic fake provider, a per-trial emitter, the bound aggregation, a thin `cmd/olaitan-fr55` CLI, and the Story-5.5 Python consumer wiring (`analyse.build_fr55_rows` now READS the trials). The harness DRIVES the adversarial-prompted chain over benign packages with `rule_severity = baseline_max_deviation = 0` and a fake provider returning the MAXIMAL raw confidence (100), then asserts the algebraic trust-bound EMPIRICALLY: zero trials past SUSPICIOUS, max ThreatScore `<= 10.5` (Claude cap 35) / `<= 7.5` (Ollama cap 25), and `olaitan_llm_cap_violation_total` == 0.
- **THE CODE-ONLY-DEFER FENCE (BI-2, the user's load-bearing scope decision):** 5.6's done-ness is the HARNESS + the OFFLINE deterministic proof (fake provider, NO cluster, NO network). The REAL 100-trial run against the live Claude/Ollama providers needs the 3-node cluster the user provisions later and is a DEFERRED carry-forward, NOT part of this story's done-ness (mirrors Story 5.5 BI-7 / the A1 RSLT-full-kind-e2e gate convention). The harness DEGRADES HONESTLY: the offline fake set is labelled `provider: fake` / `fr55-offline-` run-id prefix, NEVER a thesis-final 0/100 number; `build_fr55_rows` keeps its honest `n=0` skip until trials exist. The offline bound-proof result (the data-path proof that ships now): max ThreatScore = 10.5 (Claude shape) / 7.5 (Ollama shape), 0 of N past SUSPICIOUS, cap-violation 0, FSM state CLEAN on every trial (10.5 / 7.5 < SUSPICIOUS 20), across both RSL and RSLT-full configs.
- **OQ resolutions (the orchestrator's decisions, implemented):** OQ1 = the reserved `runs/fr55/<provider>-<config>/` sub-tree with `metadata.yaml` (join keys `scenario: benign-adversarial`, `config`, `manifest_sha256` + the FR55 summary fields) + a sibling `trials.jsonl` (`fr55.trial.v1` per-trial records); `_known_grid` extended (belt-and-suspenders) so a `benign-adversarial` cell is never orphaned. OQ2 = a NEW `internal/eval/fr55/` package + a thin `cmd/olaitan-fr55/` CLI (the cluster-driven `cmd/olaitan-eval` Runner loop is NOT contorted; the frozen `analyst.Chain` + `score.Calculator` + `provider.Provider` seams are reused). OQ3 = a small purpose-built deterministic fake provider in the FR55 package returning MAXIMAL `raw_confidence` on benign input (the unexported analyst-test stub is not importable across the package boundary). OQ4 = the final FSM state is derived from the PRODUCTION `internal/response/fsm` cumulative-band mapping, sourced from the `internal/config` threshold constants (SUSPICIOUS 20, the next threshold RESTRICTED 40), and `TestThresholds_MatchProductionConstants` pins them so "zero past SUSPICIOUS" is asserted against the real mapping. OQ5 = 5.6 DID extend `analyse.build_fr55_rows` in THIS PR to READ the `trials.jsonl` records and produce the bound-proof row, proven end-to-end with the committed offline fake-provider trials; the byte-deterministic golden was regenerated (the FR55 section now populates from the offline trials instead of skipping at `n=0`; a single-row diff in `summary.csv` + `summary.md`).
- **THE FROZEN-SEAM DISCIPLINE (BI-5, consumed READ-ONLY):** the harness CONSUMES `analyst.CapConfidence` / `analyst.GuardCappedConfidence` / `score.Calculator.Score` without modifying any of them, the weights, or the cap defaults. A failing offline bound is a PRODUCTION DEFECT to escalate (the CLI exits non-zero, the test fails loudly), never patched around. The benign generator REUSES the production `schema.EvidencePackage` (empty `RuleMatches` -> `maxSeverity == 0`; empty `BaselineDeviations` -> `maxSigma == 0`), so the SAME score path the production system runs is exercised (BI-4); `TestBenignPackage_NoDeterministicSignal` pins `Rules == 0 && Baseline == 0`.
- **THE PROMPT-ISOLATION (BI-3, changelog gate stays green):** the adversarial L1/L2/Senior prompts are Go string literals in `internal/eval/fr55/prompts.go`, injected at RUNTIME via the FROZEN Story-3.13 `analyst.Chain.SetPrompts` hot-swap seam with a harness-only `fr55-adversarial-v1` version. They are NOT under `internal/agent/prompts/defaults/*.txt`, so the NFR41 `hack/check-prompt-changelog.sh` gate (which watches ONLY that dir) stays green, and they are unreachable from any production deploy path. (BI-1: the `claude-api` skill was read before authoring the provider-facing prompts; the prompts go through the existing `provider.Prompt` abstraction, no new transport.)
- **THE METRIC-NAME DRIFT (BI-8):** the harness asserts the ACTUAL registered counter `olaitan_llm_cap_violation_total` (`analyst.CapViolationMetricName`), NOT the epic AC4's drifted `olaitan_decision_llm_cap_violation_total` (a transcription drift; Story 3.15 renamed the production metric to the FR50-canonical `olaitan_llm_cap_violation_total`, no shipped dashboard depended on the old name). The production metric is NOT renamed.
- **RSL vs RSLT-full chain wiring:** RSL = single-LLM Standard mode -> the chart sets `l2_enabled=false`, so L1 acts as the effective single-LLM analyst -> `NewChain(l1, nil, nil)` -> `ChainModeL1Only`, capped at the L1 provider's ScoreCap. RSLT-full = the full L1 -> L2 -> Senior chain -> `NewChain(l1, l2, senior)` -> `ChainModeFull`, capped at the Senior provider's ScoreCap. The bound is read from `provider.ScoreCap()` (BI-6), never hard-coded 35/25; the offline test parameterises the fake's cap to exercise both 10.5 and 7.5.
- **DEFERRED CARRY-FORWARD (BI-2):** the real 100-trial run against the REAL Claude/Ollama providers (the pre-registered N=100 per LLM config, `analysis/preregistration.md`) needs the 3-node cluster the user provisions later. It is tracked here as a carry-forward (mirroring the A1 RSLT-full-kind-e2e gate convention): the same `runs/fr55/<provider>-<config>/` artefact shape, `build_fr55_rows` reads it unchanged, and the offline `provider: fake` set is replaced by the real `provider: claude` / `provider: ollama` numbers. This is NOT part of 5.6's done-ness.
- **eval_run_ids rationale:** `n/a`. 5.6 ships the HARNESS + the OFFLINE fake-provider bound proof; the real 100-trial Claude/Ollama numbers land later on the cluster (the deferred carry-forward above), so there are no real eval run ids to cite yet.
- **ROUND-2 EMITTER-COLLISION FIX (Regression Hunter MEDIUM + Completeness Verifier LOW N1, applied on PR #75):** the offline emitter built the run-dir as `fr55-offline-fake-<config>` with the provider transport label forced to the literal `fake`, so `--provider claude` and `--provider ollama` for the SAME config both emitted to one un-shaped dir and silently overwrote each other; the round-1 patch had hand-relabelled the ollama fixtures to a name the CLI could never produce, masking the bug and leaving non-reproducible fixtures. The fix adds an emulated provider-SHAPE field (`ProviderShape` "claude"/"ollama", DISTINCT from the honest `fake` transport) to `RunOptions` / `BoundSummary`, threads it from the CLI `--provider` flag through the harness into the emitter, and encodes it in the run-dir name and run_id (`fr55-offline-fake-<shape>-<config>`) via the single-source-of-truth `fr55.RunDirName`; the metadata keeps `provider: fake` (the transport is genuinely fake, BI-2) and ADDS a `shape:` line so the path is collision-free and self-describing. All four fixtures were RE-EMITTED through `cmd/olaitan-fr55 --offline` (NO hand-relabelling) into `fr55-offline-fake-{claude,ollama}-{rsl,rslt-full}/` and are byte-identical to a fresh CLI emission (the reproducibility property a reviewer can regenerate); the old un-shaped + hand-relabelled dirs were deleted. Regression guards: `TestEmit_ShapeDistinguishesProviders` (co-emits claude+ollama into one dir, asserts both survive with their distinct 10.5/7.5 bounds) and `TestRun_OfflineShapeDistinctDirs` (CLI level). The golden was regenerated byte-identical (the FR55 value string groups by bound, not run_id/dir, so the row content is unchanged: `0/40 past SUSPICIOUS; max@7.5=7.5<=7.5, max@10.5=10.5<=10.5; cap_violations=0`). Round-2 verdicts PASS: `go build`/`go test ./internal/eval/fr55/... ./cmd/olaitan-fr55/...` green incl. the no-collision tests, `golangci-lint` 0 issues, `mypy --strict` clean, `pytest analysis/tests/` 78 passed, golden byte-deterministic twice.
- **Merge SHA:** `0e8aeda` (PR [#75](https://github.com/olokotoh/olaitan/pull/75), squash-merged to `epic-5-staging` 2026-06-16). Branch `epic-5/story-5-6-adversarial-llm-bound-test-fr55` off `epic-5-staging` (base `d53f09c`). Two bmad-code-review rounds (Copilot not configured on this repo). Round 1 (Blind Hunter + Edge Case Hunter + Acceptance Auditor + Trust-Bound/Methods): all 6 ACs MET, BOUND-PROOF verified REAL (the fake provider returns raw_confidence 100 and the production cap is the only thing holding ThreatScore at 10.5/7.5; mutation-probed - removing the cap or drifting the FSM thresholds fails the test); 1 MEDIUM + LOWs applied in `4a60397` (per-provider bound enforcement in build_fr55_rows, an Ollama 7.5 fixture, a metadata-vs-trials cross-check, doc notes). Round 2 (Regression Hunter + Completeness Verifier): Completeness PASS (6/6 findings fixed, 6/6 ACs, mypy --strict clean + 78 pytest + go green + golden deterministic, the per-run bound mutation-proven); Regression Hunter CLEAN on the patches but found a pre-existing MEDIUM harness defect - the offline emitter wrote `fr55-offline-fake-<config>` with no provider shape, so claude and ollama for one config collided and silently overwrote each other - fixed in `5c9d938` (the run-dir/run_id now encodes the emulated provider shape `fr55-offline-fake-<shape>-<config>`, all four offline fixtures re-emitted CLI-reproducibly, two no-collision regression tests added). CI green (go, the no-cluster `analysis` job, helm, docker, e2e, traceability, prompt-changelog) on the merge SHA. **DEFERRED: the real 100-trial Claude/Ollama run (pre-registered N=100 per LLM config) needs the 3-node cluster; same artefact shape, `build_fr55_rows` reads it unchanged with `provider: fake` replaced by real provider labels, and the per-provider bound check now correctly enforces 10.5 (Claude) and 7.5 (Ollama) on the mixed pool.**

### `c3.9.5-analysis-pipeline` (Story 5.5)

- **Story:** 5.5 (Statistical analysis pipeline). The FIRST Python in this otherwise-Go repo: `analysis/analyse.py` + a typed helper lib (`analysis/lib/`), a pytest suite, committed deterministic fixtures, and a NEW no-cluster `analysis` CI job. A PURE-PYTHON, NO-CLUSTER, OFFLINE story: it CONSUMES the merged Story-5.4 per-run artefacts under `runs/<run_id>/` read-only (`metadata.yaml` + the four `.jsonl` artefacts), calls NO provider, defines NO Go schema, and changes NO `internal/`/Helm behaviour (BI-16). It produces, per `(config, scenario)` cell, Detection Rate / MTTD / FPR / ATT&CK Cohen's kappa to `summary.csv` + `summary.md` (AC1), runs the pre-registered inferential tests READ FROM `analysis/preregistration.md` (AC2/AC3), attaches the `manifest_sha256` + sample-size + test provenance triad to every row (AC4), and is mypy-clean + pytest-covered + smoke-tested (AC5).
- **FIRST PYTHON IN A GO REPO (BI-1):** the dependency set is the minimal honest one for the pre-registered statistics, every version PINNED (`analysis/requirements.txt` + `analysis/requirements-dev.txt`): pandas (AC1 aggregation), scipy (Kruskal-Wallis / Wilcoxon / the one-sided Poisson rate test), statsmodels (McNemar + `multipletests`), scikit-posthocs (Dunn's-with-Holm), pingouin (ICC(2,k); OQ2 chose the library over a hand-rolled variance-component ICC for correctness), PyYAML; dev: mypy + pytest + pandas-stubs + types-PyYAML. The toolchain is wired into a NEW no-cluster `analysis` CI job (`actions/setup-python@v5` python 3.12 -> `pip install` pinned deps -> `mypy --strict analysis/` -> `pytest analysis/tests/`), structured like the existing standalone go/helm jobs (NO kind cluster, NO `services:`, NO label gate; pure offline analysis proven on EVERY PR). `make analysis` / `make analysis-test` mirror it locally.
- **METRIC -> ARTEFACT-FIELD MAPPING (BI-3), CONSUMED not re-derived:** Detection Rate = `mean(success_criterion_met)` (Story-5.4's honest floor-aware verdict, NOT re-derived from `fsm.jsonl`); MTTD = median + mean of `measured_time_to_detect` EXCLUDING the `-1` `NeverDetectedSentinel`; FPR = benign-sweep `fsm.jsonl` `after_state` escalations past CLEAN / wall-window hours (`n/a` when no benign runs, BI-5); ATT&CK kappa = `assessments.jsonl` predicted technique vs the Story-5.2 scenario ground-truth (S1->T1611 ... S5->T1496; `n/a` for F/RS / empty assessments, BI-6).
- **THE HONESTY FENCES (BI-4, BI-7):** the `fsm_state_source` partition (`n_observed_transitions` vs `n_detection_signal_only`) is reported per cell so a detection-signal floor is never read as a confirmed high-state attainment (BI-4). The pipeline DEGRADES HONESTLY on the missing/partial run-set: the real 400 runs DO NOT EXIST YET (they land later on the cluster, Story 5.9); the pipeline reports `n` per cell + test, emits `n/a`/NaN (never a fabricated number) when `n == 0`, flags `underpowered: true` below the pre-registered N (15 main / 10 ablation), SKIPS a test with `status: skipped (insufficient data, n=...)` (never a crash, never a fabricated p-value) when its inputs are absent, and labels the synthetic fixture run-set `fixture: true` (NEVER thesis-final, BI-7).
- **PREREG ALIGNMENT (BI-2) + the updated FPR framing:** the test choices, alphas, and correction families are READ FROM `analysis/preregistration.md` Section 8a; 5.5 ENFORCES, it does not re-choose. The four-way DR McNemar family corrects to 0.05/5 = 0.01; the ablation family to 0.05/10 = 0.005; the FPR equivalence family to 0.05/2 = 0.025. DEVIATION-FROM-STORY-PROSE (recorded per BI-2, prereg wins): the 5.5 story prose mentioned "Poisson regression" and "alpha 0.05 two-sided" for FPR; the COMMITTED prereg (revised after the story was written) reframes RQ3/FPR as a ONE-SIDED Poisson rate test (TOST-style non-inferiority against the 2-escalations/hour bound), family = the 2 LLM-bearing configs (RSL, RSLT-full). 5.5 implements the prereg's one-sided framing. The corrected alpha per test is read from the registry's correction-family column (BI-14).
- **THE EXPLORATORY:TRUE ENFORCEMENT (BI-14):** the registry parser (`lib/prereg.py`) strips the `<a id=...></a>` anchor + backticks to recover the bare `test_id` and matches membership by EXACT STRING on the whole id (the opaque ids `RQ4-FR55-BOUND` / `RQ5-ICC` are never field-parsed); any emitted `test_id` not in the registry is stamped `exploratory: true` in a separate Markdown section + CSV column.
- **McNEMAR PAIRING (BI-8, REVISED round 1 / H2):** the four-way + ablation McNemar comparisons are PAIRED over the deterministic scenario-instance index (instance i of config A paired with instance i of config B over the same Story-5.2 deterministic stimulus). Pairing is by an INNER JOIN on a shared instance KEY, not positional `min(n_A, n_B)` truncation: positional truncation mis-paired whenever a verdict was NA, and because the real `run_id` is timestamp-first (`<UTC>-<scenario>-<config>-<hash>`, `cmd/olaitan-eval/main.go:418`) positional order is wall-clock order, not instance order, so it mis-paired even with no NAs. The key is the explicit `scenario_instance` metadata field (Story 5.9 populates it per replicate; the committed fixtures carry it), falling back to the `run_id` `-NN` suffix; a run with neither, or NA on either side, is dropped from the pair honestly (BI-7). A pair is included only when BOTH sides are present and non-NA; the paired `n` is reported. The deterministic-stimulus assumption (instance i of a scenario is the SAME stimulus across configs) is the contract this pairing rests on (documented in `io.scenario_instance_key`).
- **CROSS-LANGUAGE FSM-RANK COUPLING (L2):** `analysis/lib/fsm.py` hand-mirrors the Go state ordering from `internal/schema/state.go` (StateRank) for the FPR escalation predicate. There is NO automated cross-language check; a future state addition or rename on the Go side must be mirrored into `_STATE_RANK` by hand, or the benign-sweep escalation count will silently disagree with the FSM (the Go file is authoritative).
- **ATT&CK kappa is POOLED per config, not per cell (round-1 review M3):** the metric row is `attack_kappa_pooled` with `scenario=all`, one row per config (F/RS `n/a`). Kappa is computed over all of a config's scenario runs so the ground-truth technique label varies across S1-S5 (T1611/T1552/T1613/T1071/T1496); a per-`(config, scenario)` kappa is structurally degenerate because the truth is constant within a single scenario. FORWARD NOTE: any later Chapter 4 consumer keys off `attack_kappa_pooled` (scenario `all`), not a per-cell kappa row.
- **Paired-test instance key (round-1 review H2):** McNemar and the ablation contributions pair on a shared `scenario_instance` key via an inner join (not positional truncation), so the partial run-set and the real timestamp-first `run_id` ordering cannot mis-pair. FORWARD NOTE: Story 5.9 MUST populate `scenario_instance` per replicate on every real run (the fixtures carry it; the `-NN` run_id-suffix fallback does not match the real `<ts>-<scenario>-<config>-<hash>` shape), or the paired tests degrade honestly to a smaller n.
- **Determinism (BI-13):** no wall-clock in the output, sorted iteration over runs/cells/tests, a seeded RNG for the ablation bootstrap CI; the smoke test asserts byte-identical `summary.csv` + `summary.md` against a committed golden (`analysis/fixtures/golden/`) on every run.
- **Provenance (§3.9.5 story-number-aligned):** the claim is `c3.9.5-analysis-pipeline` at §3.9.5 (VERIFIED FREE: the matrix carried c3.9.1/c3.9.2/c3.9.3/c3.9.4/c3.9.8 only, no §3.9.5; §3.9.5 was RESERVED for Story 5.5 by the c3.9.8 row). It keeps the §3.9.x = Story-5.x mapping (5.5 -> §3.9.5). `report/chapter-3-methodology.md` lives in the thesis repo, not here; per the §3.9.1-§3.9.4 slug-continuity precedent, this row attaches the §3.9.5 anchor in trust until the chapter section lands.
- **eval_run_ids rationale:** `n/a`. 5.5 ships the PIPELINE + FIXTURES + TESTS; the real 400-run thesis numbers are produced LATER on the cluster (Story 5.9), so there are no eval run ids to cite yet. The forward contract (Story 5.8 AC4): each later Chapter 4 number cites its `manifest_hash` + `n` + `test` provenance row (this pipeline's output) alongside the prereg `test_id` + the eval run ids when those land.
- **Merge SHA:** `b20c01e` (PR [#74](https://github.com/olokotoh/olaitan/pull/74), squash-merged to `epic-5-staging` 2026-06-16). Branch `epic-5/story-5-5-statistical-analysis-pipeline` off `epic-5-staging` (base `b21477f`). Two bmad-code-review rounds (Copilot not configured on this repo). Round 1 (Blind Hunter + Edge Case Hunter + Acceptance Auditor + Statistics/Methods): all 5 ACs MET; 2 HIGH / 4 MEDIUM / 4 LOW, all 10 applied in `a74483c` (H2 McNemar/ablation keyed inner-join pairing; H1 jsonl error context; M4 real manifest_hash on inferential/ablation rows via provenance.for_runs; M3 ATT&CK kappa pooled per config; M2 FPR per-run window accumulation; M1 off-grid-run reconciliation; L1-L4 fixture-flag/p-formatting/docstring/FSM-coupling). Round 2 (Regression Hunter + Completeness Verifier): Completeness PASS (10/10 resolved, 5/5 ACs, mypy --strict clean 22 files, pytest 67 passed, golden deterministic); Regression Hunter CLEAN (fixes verified, new known-answer tests mutation-proven, BI-7 no-fabrication holds), 2 LOW doc-debt swept in `17dbbc3`. CI green (the no-cluster `analysis` job [mypy --strict + pytest], go, helm, docker, e2e, traceability, prompt-changelog) on the merge SHA. **FORWARD: Story 5.9 must populate `scenario_instance` per replicate on real runs (the paired tests rely on it); the 400-run thesis numbers land later on the cluster (this story ships the pipeline + fixtures + tests, never thesis-final numbers, BI-7).**
